Title: Secunda Remonstrantia Ministrorum Ecclesiarum Hollandicarum et West-Frisicarum qui Remonstrantes vocantur, Illustrissimis DD. Ordinibus Hollandiae et West-Frisiae tradita in Aprili anno 1617; cum Secunda Contra-Remonstrantia seu Responsio ad eam, Reformatorum earundem Ecclesiarum Ministrorum, in qua demonstratur Remonstrantes novam doctrinam in Ecclesias Reformatas introducere, iisdem Illustrissimis DD. Ordinibus tradita in Augusto anno 1617; ex Belgicis in Latinam linguam fideliter translatae (title page read in the OCR)
Creator: The Remonstrant ministers of Holland and West Friesland (the Second Remonstrance; the host catalogue names Jan Uytenbogaert's 'Copye van seker vertooch' as the Dutch original) and the Contra-Remonstrant ministers (the Responsio; the host catalogue names Jacobus Trigland's 'Klaer ende grondich teghen-vertoogh' and Jacobus Taurinus); the Latin translator from the Dutch is not named
Publisher: Lugduni-Batavorum (Leiden): apud Johannem Orlers (imprint read in the OCR at file line 71)
Publication Date: 1617 (title page and imprint read in the OCR)
Rights: Public Domain (a 1617 print; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item secundaremonstra00uyte, file secundaremonstra00uyte_djvu.txt (John Adams Library at the Boston Public Library copy, John Adams former owner; collections johnadamsBPL, bostonpubliclibrary, americana), fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, second-pass agent for the continental Reformed and Scandinavian worlds), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: none on the item (no possible-copyright-status field and no licence field; not access-restricted); rights rest on the 1617 title-page date.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (no characters altered; the raw file contains 0 long-s glyphs, so no long-s normalisation was done). Rights basis restated: a 1617 print; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule.
Content note: Both sides of the quarrel at its height, in their own formal words to the States of Holland: the Remonstrants' second petition of April 1617 and the Contra-Remonstrants' answer of August 1617, put into Latin from the Dutch for readers outside the Netherlands in the same year. Contents as the OCR shows them: the title page (file lines 25-71), the Secunda Remonstrantia, then RESPONSIO REFORMATORUM ECCLESIARUM MINISTRORUM (the Contra-Remonstrantia) from file line 943, with running heads 'II. Contra-Remonstrantia' and page numbers to the end (about line 7500). The Dutch originals named by the host catalogue are not vendored and were not found on the host by the first pass. A primary original-language witness for both parties, to be rendered by Opus under the September 2026 ruling once a passage is verified against the page image. OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 percent (about 0.47 of tokens read as words against the project's Latin baseline, which is a patristic text; the Latin reads, with the 1617 typography's long s as f throughout, 'Remonftrantes', 'poffemus', ligatures and abbreviations misread, and the Boston Public Library's shelf stamp on the first leaves). No U+017F characters were present. SECOND WITNESS for exact wording (INTAKE scan-quality rule): locate a passage here and verify against the page image before rendering or quoting.
